# INBOX — caught, not lost

**Append-only. One line per item. Claude does not open this file unless Dann asks
for it.**

This exists so Dann can surface a thought mid-task and let it go immediately,
knowing it is held. Nothing here is scoped, costed, or ruled on. **An item leaves
this file only when Dann rules it into the tracker in `STATE.md`.** That is the
only gate, and it is what stops the backlog being generated by the process itself.

Format: `I.nn | YYYY-MM-DD | one line, in Dann's words where possible`

---

| id | date | the thought |
|---|---|---|
| I.01 | 2026-08-14 | Mobile: Notation is anchored to the bottom and opens fully expanded, so you cannot reach the metadata fields without closing it. Its open state should be closed. |
| I.02 | 2026-09-01 | "I must have dismissed this dialog box ten times. Why does it persist?" The install prompt never saves a dismissal on desktop: `InstallPrompt.svelte:76` guards the only write behind `if (isIos)`, and the iOS path writes `sessionStorage`, which dies with the tab. Every singer on a stable URL is asked again on every load, forever. Found on the 510a280 walk. **NUMBERED N.105 by Dann 2026-09-01.** |
| I.03 | 2026-09-01 | "Why is there no meter given?" Ilya draws no time signature anywhere. `packages/score-parser/src/staff-renderer.ts:964` reads it for spacing only, and the SMuFL digits at `:356-359` are spent on N.104's tacet count. Without Sun 1 draws a duplet bracket that cannot be read without its 12/8. **RULED BY DANN 2026-09-01, on the 510a280 walk: "Meter belong[s] in the loupe head, yes."** So when a metre is drawn it is emitted with the clef and key, before the first music mark, and tonight's paint-order gate carries it into the head with no new code. That is now ruled rather than inferred. **HIS RULING DOES NOT REACH A MID-SYSTEM METRE CHANGE**, which is drawn after the first music mark and belongs with the music rather than in the head; the gate would put it in the head, which would be wrong. Still open, and still his. Found on the 510a280 walk. |

- Floor-commit restore flakiness (Code, 2026-08-26, slice 1 memo): on `98bba71` the score reverted to the empty envelope in 3 of 6 automated runs; 5 of 5 held on the slice 1 tree. Unexplained by a CSS deletion; possibly the harness. Worth its own look.
- Tether 20 draft, HELD BY DANN 2026-09-01, not written into CONTRACT.md. "Before you change how a number is computed, find every other site that computes the same number, and say what depended on them agreeing." Evidence, one instance: before `510a280` the loupe's head was `Math.min(...allHits)` over every hit rectangle in the system and the window's left is `Math.min(...own)` over the held measure's (`loupe.ts:79`); the measure's set is a subset of the system's, and a subset's minimum is never smaller, so `head <= windowLeft` held by set containment and the two viewports could not overlap. **No comment said so.** Moving the head onto music ink killed the containment argument silently. Sub-points drafted: grep for the quantity rather than the identifier, since the two sites shared no name; if two things were equal and now are not, treat the difference as a defect until measured; leave the invariant in the code; and in a memo, put quantities that meet in one table, because two numbers four pages apart are two numbers nobody compares. **HELD because it would be a twentieth tether in a session that began by diagnosing that this project's governance accretes and never retires, and because the desk has ONE instance of the class.** Reconsider it when the retirement pass runs and names something to remove. The desk's proposed one-in-one-out rule is also unruled.
- N.105 (numbered by Dann 2026-09-01, unplaced): the install prompt never saves a dismissal, so "Not now" lasts exactly one page view. `InstallPrompt.svelte:73-77` guards its only write behind `if (isIos)`, so on desktop `dismiss()` sets `visible = false` and stores nothing; the next load fires `beforeinstallprompt` again and `:61` shows the banner eight seconds later. The iOS path writes `sessionStorage`, which dies with the tab. **This reaches singers, not just the walk**: anyone on the stable URL who declines is asked again on every load, in perpetuity. Dann hit it ten times in one hour on the 510a280 walk. Fix direction: persist the decline durably and per-origin, and rule how long a decline lasts before Ilya may ask again. NOT ESTABLISHED: whether a declined native Chrome prompt (`install()`, outcome `dismissed`) should count as a decline too.

The item: make intake legible to a new user, states named, nothing silent.

- N.103 (numbered by Dann 2026-08-27): measures do not re-space when ink is added, walking the dot on dae29f5: the renderer lays notes at fixed x from the read, so an augmentation dot is drawn into space belonging to the next note and collides with its accidental. Adding a dot, an accidental, or any widening correction wants the measure re-spaced, which is real engraving work (Gould on horizontal spacing, minimum distances, accidental columns) and NOT a marker or z-order problem. Wants a number and a slice.
- N.104 (numbered by Dann 2026-08-27): the page must represent measures with no voice content. A tacet stretch shows as a single consolidated rest with the number of silent measures above it, per Gould (VERIFY: the priors memo records rest geometry, pp. 34-38, as never photographed, so her multibar-rest rules are probably NOT held; report NOT ESTABLISHED and fall back on engraving convention rather than inventing). This is the honest resolution of the measure-number disagreement Dann found on 2026-08-27 (loupe read m. 3 on the visibly second bar, consistent with one piano-only bar before the voice enters): do not renumber the tag, complete the page. A singer cannot count an entry from a page that hides the bars they must count. CONFIRMED by Code 2026-08-27, so do not re-measure: the Bass part's <measure number="1"> carries 0 notes, 0 rests, 0 lyrics; the Piano's carries 5; the page draws hit ids m1 through m17 with no m0; the tag's number is copied verbatim from the source (musicxml-parser.ts:599, read at +page.svelte:1102), so the TAG IS FAITHFUL and the page is short one bar. Also carried: the first system declares data-system="0-3" while drawing source indices 1-4, so the loupe's systemIndexOf compares two scales that differ by that same bar; it reads correctly today and wants a look when N.104 changes what is drawn. Gould holds NOTHING on rest geometry (priors memo line 220, pp. 34-38 unphotographed), so the multibar rest's dimensions are either photographed or named as convention and recorded as convention, the way the tie's 0.40 was recorded as Dann's eye.

- 2026-09-12, AMENDS the STATE.md entry of 2026-08-29 under RULINGS DANN OWES ("the loupe draws the singer's words in a different typeface than the page does"). Mechanism established by reading tonight, not a container override as that entry says: the renderer's own SVG root declares `font-family="'Source Serif 4', Georgia, serif"` (`staff-renderer.ts:2827`), and `paginateScore` STRIPS that opening tag when it nests each system (`page-layout.ts:376`). The page's own root (`page-layout.ts:364`) and system wrapper (`:374`) declare no font, so the Cyrillic text (`staff-renderer.ts:2697`, no font of its own) inherits `body`'s `var(--font-sans)` (`app.css:200-201`). The loupe re-declares the serif on its wrapper (`Loupe.svelte:884`, `:897`), so it keeps the renderer's intent. THE PAGE IS THE DEVIATION, and nobody chose sans. Fix is one attribute on `page-layout.ts:364`. Which face the Cyrillic wears is Dann's, unruled.

- 2026-09-12, DEFECT, NUMBERED N.128 by Dann the same evening and promoted to STATE.md; brief written at docs/sessions/brief-n128-stale-beat_r1_2026-09-12.md. A CORRECTED DURATION DOES NOT MOVE THE FOLLOWING NOTES' STORED BEAT, SO BEAM GROUPING GOES STALE. Found on Dann's own Sunless 01 page, system 1, where « на–я » is flagged and « ла–я » is beamed although both are drawn as two eighths in the last beat. MEASURED from the live SVG in Dann's browser: spacing identical for both pairs (тес→на 54.78 px = ми→ла 54.78 px; на→я 20.14 vs ла→я 19.67; я→rest 15.68 vs 15.69); all four stems down, so timbre did not break the group; the only eighth rests (SMuFL e4e6) sit at x 353.2, 490.85, 602.5, each AFTER the second « я », so no rest or barline falls between either pair; the system's single beam runs x 563.89 to 583.56. CAUSE: the beam key is `measure | beatIndexOf(ev.rhythmicPosition.fraction, ts) | timbre` (`staff-renderer.ts:1611`), and `modification-engine.ts` has ZERO references to `rhythmicPosition` (grep, 0 hits). A duration correction changes the duration and the x layout advances by duration, but no following event's rhythmicPosition is recomputed. On the page на is event `m1-1-1` (1/1 whole = 4.0 quarters, beat 3 of 12/8) while я is `m1-5-4` (5.0 quarters, beat 4): different beats, no beam. The other pair is `m2-9-8` (4.5) and `m2-5-4` (5.0), both beat 4, so it beams. Corroborating: the source file (`~/Downloads/Mussorgsky - Sunless 01 - Within Four Walls (engraved).musicxml`, md5 265f7cb5fa359942b54826795cf10c4f, byte-identical to `apps/web/src/lib/score/ingestion/fixtures/sunless-01-engraved.musicxml`) has тес as a PLAIN quarter at 3.0q, while the page draws it 1.5 quarters wide. WIDER SCOPE, NOT VERIFIED: two other consumers compare the same stale field, `sustain.ts:67-79` and `watchlist.ts:232-240`, both matching a note against a tempo or marking position, so a corrected duration may mis-assign a sustain marking or a watch-list entry as well.
- 2026-09-12, FINDING from Code's loupe-typeface memo, DESERVES A NUMBER (desk recommends N.129, Dann to rule). THE SCORE'S UNDERLAY IS SPACED IN THE WRONG FONT'S METRICS. `underlay-widths.ts:690` declares "Per-1000-em advance widths for Source Serif 4 Cyrillic (measured at 12.5px)", and the renderer uses that table for the syllable column advance (`staff-renderer.ts:754-762`) and for hyphen and extender endpoints (`:2742-2750`). The page has drawn those glyphs in Source Sans 3 since the paginator began stripping the renderer's serif root (`page-layout.ts:376`), so every syllable's spacing and every hyphen/extender end on Score markup has been computed from metrics the glyphs never had. Code measured the gap at about 5% on one word: « ночь » 27.72 px serif against 26.34 px sans. Desk read `underlay-widths.ts:690` and both renderer sites itself, 2026-09-12. Tonight's loupe change did NOT create this and does not worsen it: the loupe clones the page's layout rather than re-measuring, so there is one error, in the layout, not two. Candidate fixes, unruled: remeasure the Cyrillic table in Source Sans 3, or make the face a parameter so the table and the drawn font cannot diverge again. Bears directly on N.118 (a trailing comma inherits the same error) and on the `columnAdvance` crowding item already in STATE.md's OWED list.

- 2026-09-12, CAUSE ESTABLISHED for the empty poem box beside a fully drawn score, and it closes the walk finding recorded earlier this evening. **N.121's ruling of 2026-09-10 is NOT BUILT.** That ruling: "A score arriving with words into an EMPTY box fills the box and tags the poem receipt 'from score'." Read by the desk 2026-09-12: `doc.inputText` is assigned in exactly three places, `+page.svelte:2679` (the field's own input), `:2403` (clear), and `document.svelte.ts:269` (restore from the stored record). **No path anywhere fills the poem box from an ingested score's lyrics.** The stored record does hold the poem (`library/types.ts:80`, restored at `library/library.ts:65`), so the storage is not the gap; the poem was never written in the first place. The syllables on Dann's page come from the score file's own underlay, which is why Score markup is full while the poem box and the whole Transcription document are empty: Transcription draws from `lines`, built by transcribing `doc.inputText`. Dann's words, 2026-09-12: "If there is text, it should be fed through the Transcribe pipeline, and we might as well capture it as a formatting Transcription." THE HARD PART, and it is why this may have stalled: a publisher's underlay is syllabified (Ком, нат, ка as separate seats), so filling the box means rejoining words rather than pasting the syllable stream. Feasible: the file's own division is parsed and available as `ev.syllable?.type` (`pairings.ts:713-715`), so `begin` through `end` concatenates. Brief to be written under N.121, not a new cardinal.

- 2026-09-13, SELECTED BY DANN, NOT ADOPTED. A warm brown for the colour story, should one ever be wanted. His words: "Umber is the best selection... I'm not sure whether I want to use it or not, but I wanted the option." NOTHING IS ASSIGNED TO IT and no destination uses it; it is on the shelf. **UMBER, OKLCH hue 65°, built exactly as the four existing families are built:** light (desk) `#D7CCC0`, medium (band) `#9C7F62`, dark (chip, one step down) `#8E6F51` at 4.62 on white, and the fifth value rose also needed, label ink for small caps on cream `#7A5D40` at 5.09 (lighter alternative `#806245` at 4.71). Proposed names, unruled: `--warm-umber` follows the two-word pattern, `--quiet-umber` echoes cobalt's adjective. ESTABLISHED while deriving it, and worth keeping: **the light tier is literally 40 % hue plus 60 % white**, verified against all four families, every hex reproducing exactly; the dark tier was hand-set per family to clear 4.5 on white, which is why no single multiplier fits it. Existing hue angles for spacing: rose 19°, sage 130°, cobalt 262°, lavender 310°; umber at 65° is 46° from rose and 65° from sage. Rejected candidates and why: chestnut 45° reads as a warmer rose at only 26° from it; tobacco 75° starts to read olive. Drawing with all four candidates, the reference families, and umber in situ: `docs/sessions/drawing-warm-brown_r1_2026-09-13.html`.
- 2026-09-13, RULED BY DANN: the colour families take one-word names. **sage, rose, lavender, cobalt, umber.** So `--dusty-rose` becomes `--rose`, `--deeper-lavender` becomes `--lavender`, `--quiet-cobalt` becomes `--cobalt`; `--sage` is already right and does not move; umber joins the set if and when it is adopted. He chose "rose" over "pink" when the desk noted that `#A67B7B` is a muted brownish rose and "pink" would set a brighter expectation. NO HEX VALUE CHANGES. Counted by the desk 2026-09-13: `--dusty-rose` 25 uses in 12 files, `--deeper-lavender` 49 in 22, `--quiet-cobalt` 21 in 15, about 95 call sites. Tests assert hex literals, not token names, so no gate can break from the rename alone. DESK DEFAULT, unopposed: the pacifier's `--muted-lavender` and `--light-lavender` stay as they are, because they name values inside a family rather than families. Brief: `docs/sessions/brief-colour-token-rename_r1_2026-09-13.md`.
- 2026-09-13, RULED BY DANN. **THE COLOUR STORY, SETTLED. Transcribe into PRODUCT.md at the next touch.** The principle, in his framing and corrected by the tree: **a hue names ONE IDEA, and a band takes the hue of the idea it holds, wherever that band appears.** A hue travelling into another room is not a collision; it is the system working. Evidence, read 2026-09-13: `Drawer.svelte:1187` paints the Input band with `var(--lang-chip-transcription)` and carries Dann's own N.108-5 ruling in the comment, *"painted sage like Text (hue names place: it is text)"*, so sage already travels into Score markup's drawer and always has; `:1178` paints the Piece band with `var(--lang-chip-guide)`, so cobalt already travels too. THE DESK'S EARLIER CLAIM THAT COBALT-ON-PIECE WAS "THE ONLY GENUINE COLLISION IN THE SYSTEM" WAS WRONG and is withdrawn; it proposed a neutral Piece band on that wrong basis and Dann was right to resist. THE MAP: **sage = the words** (Transcription, the Input band); **lavender = notation and voice** (Score markup, its band, the turning layer, the pacifier); **cobalt = information** (Guide, the Piece band) — Piece STAYS COBALT, ruled; **rose = your voice, explained** (Insights); **umber = the book** (Learn). THE ONLY MOVE THE REFORM REQUIRES: **Learn goes from rose to umber**, because rose was carrying two unrelated ideas, which is why `--surround-insights` had to be minted as a second name for `#DBCACA`. Everything else stays where it is. Umber's values are in the 2026-09-13 umber line above, pending the four-value ramp decision. SEQUENCING, desk's note: the band/chip/ink spacing proposal (steps of 0.11 instead of 0.05) is NOT ruled, and umber's three marks depend on it, so ruling the ramp before building Learn's umber avoids building it twice.
- 2026-09-13, RULED BY DANN, two small ones. (a) The French for the drawer band `MARKUP` is « Annotation », SINGULAR. - 2026-09-19, **STANDING AUTHORITY FOR FRENCH MUSICAL TERMINOLOGY: Roberge's GDRM**, alongside the OQLF, which governs punctuation by Dann's ruling of 2026-08-21. **First use, the same day:** it settled *m.* against *mes.* The entry at `08-abrev.htm` gives « mes. » as the French abbreviation and states that **m.** and **mm.** are the abbreviations of the ENGLISH *measure*, with German taking **T.** So *m.* in a French string is an anglicism, not a shorter French form. `04-notes.htm` adds that prose writes *mesure* out in full and the abbreviation is for parenthetical and reference use, which is what Ilya's measure tag and witness line are. **Read the entry verbatim; a summary of that page stated the opposite.**

- 2026-09-16, Dann RULED (build, unplaced): Ilya operates on a complete set of Grayson's ten sung vowels. Bozeman's values are surrogate data until the singer offers a reliable sample: no vowel is ever blank or without pitch-of-turning information. Any gap is filled from them, flagged, placed on a continuum between Bozeman's charts by the singer's samples (Dann 2026-09-16: no single chart is picked); Dann 2026-09-16: values are derived mathematically around whatever samples exist, forming a hybrid of sung, derived, and surrogate values. Ilya's derivation ratios (derivations.ts:19-35) reproduce Mitton 2020 Table 5.3 within 2 Hz, i.e. they are fitted to one voice. Amends Kimi 2026-07-11 (self-declaration the only routing input, plausibility.ts:34-38): Dann 2026-09-16, a declared voice type plays NO part in choosing values, because fach is bound up with identity and Ilya makes no pronouncements on a singer's identity; the matched chart is internal and its voice-type name is never shown. Open: the surrogate when too few vowels are sampled to match (the code's all-voices union bands are one candidate); what counts as a reliable sample; one number from a band; minimum samples to match; the four vowels Bozeman does not chart; fR2 (bands are fR1 only, plausibility.ts:48).

- 2026-10-02 08:46: Dann, on the loupe picture from the desk's walk: the two underlay lines differ on purpose. With Open syllables on, the IPA line moves a syllable's closing consonant to the next syllable of the word, a practice from early singing sources (he names Vaccai) that favours legato, since singers sing on the vowel. The Cyrillic line stays an accurate copy of the engraving: *"We do not want the actual orthographic text underlay to reflect open syllables."* Open syllables is a courtesy the singer switches on; it is not the default.

Desk consequence, a DESK DEFAULT with its condition, his to overrule (restated 00:07 after his reaction to the absolute words "ever" and "anything"): by default, a Send sits quietly where the singer has already opened the word or the voice, and Ilya does not prompt, count, or badge. Condition for departing: a prompt may appear where it serves the singer's own task and Dann rules that one in by name. DESK DEFAULT: transcribe into `PRODUCT.md` at the close with 'Ilya will not commercialise' (INBOX 23:54).
- 2026-10-21 00:05 Dann, on the sharing questions: *"ever and anything are scary words for me. I'm mainly interested in providing an excellent app that is useful and flexible and expandable. Sometimes that means accepting data from users. Product purpose, in his words, a candidate for `PRODUCT.md` with 'Ilya will not commercialise' (00:03) and 'the user initiates the contact' (00:03): the most robust tool the desk can make, useful, flexible, and expandable; it may accept data from users; no mailing lists; no commercialization; the aim is to remove any credible boundary to Russian vocal literature other than a foundational education in IPA. His rule of 2026-09-14 (`CONTRACT.md` tether 19) holds here: a ruling is a stated default plus the condition that would justify departing from it, never an absolute.

- 2026-10-21 21:2x, Dann: *"The dictionary and the reader are both part of the app, they don't need internet connectin to work. Verify."* (transcribed as written). Desk read (not run): the reader's models persist after first load and are read from the Cache API before any fetch (`apps/web/node_modules/homr-web/dist/models/store.js:223`, `cache.js:63`), but onnxruntime's WebAssembly (`homr-reader.ts:54`) and the metre model (`metre-model.ts:65-70`) rest on the browser's HTTP cache, since `sw.js:60` skips `/omr/`; and the DICTIONARY does not start offline: every start fetches the manifest first with `cache: 'no-cache'` (`apps/web/src/lib/loader.ts:283`, `:623`), a failure throws (`:624-628`), and the stored copy is keyed by that manifest's hash (`:631`), so the app falls to inference mode; the service worker's manifest branch (`sw.js:69`) falls back to a cache that nothing ever fills. First use of both needs the network (files served from the app's own host). Open: whether he rules in a fix (remember the last manifest hash in IndexedDB and load from it when the manifest cannot be fetched; the desk's recommendation, small, `loader.ts` only).

- 2026-11-01 23:05, Dann: "Are there implications for other auto-comments in Analysis?" (row 173). Desk read D&P pp. 76-87 in full: 3P3 palatalizes paired dentals before ANY soft consonant (p. 76), limited by four restrictions (stops т, д not across the / boundary unless geminate; dentals not before рь or soft velars; н not before soft labials; л, р never unless geminate), with dentals before soft labials palatalized (p. 80: зверь, четверть, сфере, поспеешь, разметили); p. 84: T before a soft labial optional in both styles; p. 85: "observation of the general rule 3P is recommended for most practical purposes". This contradicts `engine.ts:997-1005` (dentals only before a soft dental or м), whose comment cites D&P 3R(a) p. 86, which is the rule for р. Blurb side: `packages/blurb/src/composer.ts:184-186` sends every hard consonant to `hard:<letter>`, whose template says "no palatalizing agent nearby", including every consonant a restriction blocked before a soft one. Put to Dann as one question at 23:1x.
- 2026-11-01 23:15, Dann: "Ilya is the operationalization of Grayson's work. Grayson draws on Derwing and Priestly, but their book is a speech resource. Grayson's opinoin is more important here, but we also want to serve the user with the best information available." 23:16: "The idea of an optional softening should really come from a coach. Ilya is going to take a defensible position." (transcribed as written; option 2, optional softening, is out). Desk read Grayson 2012 in the device shell: p. 26 n. 83 (D&P, Avanesov, Jones and Ward "all advocate the liberal application of regressive palatalization throughout the consonant cluster"); p. 209 (lyric diction follows Old Muscovite, "palatalizes nearly all secondary cluster members"; five limits: л, р, н, velars, labials; none for т д с з; n. 276 credits D&P 76-87); p. 210 (сестрёнка: "entire cluster of /стр/ is palatalized"); p. 211 (симметрический "reflects OM Stage style"; lyric diction simplifies by borrowing from OM); p. 310 ("the easiest and most formal choice is to palatalize the entire cluster, except" ж ш ц, and л if not the trigger). The дверь guard (`consonants-clusters.test.ts:163-169`, commit 864a7d13, 2026-02-27) is not supported by any of these pages; Grayson's email of 2026-02-26 is quoted in `engine.ts` only, its full text NOT ESTABLISHED in project knowledge.

Yes to a one-line note where D&P's speech rules differ (p. 79), "information for the singer, not a second pronunciation". The desk's sentence "Grayson took D&P's description of the Old Muscovite style and resolved its options toward softening, as a singer's rule." belongs in the GUIDE (draft, EN and FR owed for ratification). Source found: Grayson's email to Dann, Gmail thread "Ilya, the app I created", 2026-02-25 06:09 UTC (01:09 Toronto; the code says Feb 26), from altobasso@gmail.com: "Russian \"Stage\" pronunciation (from which Russian lyric diction borrowed) palatalizes everything in similar clusters, except the \"always hard\" consonants (of course)." Dann's reply 2026-02-25 08:18 UTC: implement /sʲmʲerʲtʲ/ "as the default and figure out the implications of what you've said to understand how far it should reach." Grayson p. 206 lists the boundaries to regressive palatalization (ш ж ц; ъ except after в с з; a vowel; a new agent; the word; punctuation): рь is not among them.
- 2026-11-01 23:31, Dann asked for a LEARN explanation of the dentals in his authorial voice. Draft r1: `../sessions/draft-learn-u6-clusters-dentals_r1_2026-11-01.md` (EN; FR owed; IPA after the engine change). Desk finding at 23:4x: Grayson p. 209 rule 2 reads "Do not regressively palatalize /r/ within a cluster except ... directly following и, е, or э (in the stressed syllable only)", his three examples (смерть, терпеть, кирпич) all have a soft consonant after р, and D&P p. 87 says 3R does not apply when the following consonant is hard (четверг, первый n/c). - 2026-11-19 13:58 Dann RULED, on N.204 unit a finding D3 (transcribed as written): *"Why a bright-a in всякий? That a is not interpalatal. It should be a dark-a / Likewise некий should be an open-e because it is not interpalatal and does not raise, same with пегий. I realize the suffix changes the conditions but we need to acknowledge that and keep the logic straight"*. The ruling: where the palatalization map marks a velar hard under the adjectival stage suffix (-кий, -гий, -хий), the preceding vowel is NOT interpalatal; всякий takes a dark [ɑ], некий and пегий an open [ɛ]. The code must acknowledge the suffix in one place instead of letting a letter rule overrule the map. Cause, read by the desk this session: `engine.ts:1592-1593` computes `isFollowedByPal = softAt(palMap, …) || this.isPalatalized(followingConsonant, …)`, and `isPalatalized` (`:818-828`) returns true at `:825` for any consonant followed by е, ё, и, ю, or я, so the map's hard verdict is computed and then discarded; `:1611` then sets `interpalatal` true and `:1614` fronts the vowel. `isPalatalized` is a hand-rolled phonological predicate, which `CONTRACT.md` section 8 forbids. Its own lane, not lane 71: it moves the IPA corpus approval and needs a measure of how many words shift.

- 2026-11-22 15:0x desk FINDING, SEEN on lane 82's own after picture magnified and then read in source. ONE FIGURE SPELLS A PITCH TWO WAYS. `pitchLabel` (`note-picker.ts:155-157`) writes `D♯4` with U+266F and no space, and its own comment cites Dann's rule of 2026-10-11 04:58, the digit flush and no space before the accidental. The row label (`PitchSeconds.svelte:194`) and the passaggio caption (`:199`) both call it, but `.line-name` adds `font-style: italic` (`:235-237`) over the same `var(--font-sans)`, and the italic face does not carry U+266F, so the browser substitutes a face for that one glyph. On the page the row reads `D#4`, upright hash flush to the letter, and the caption eleven pixels away reads `D ♯4`, a slanted sharp with a gap before it. PRE-EXISTING, seen in the before picture too, so NOT lane 82's to fix. Not yet a row.

- 2026-11-22 20:13, Dann, MONOCHROME skin, the purpose and the vibe, transcribed as written: *"So my idea for the Monochrom skin is toi allow patrterns to stand in for contrast.. but I'm most interested in WCAG's recommendations for colourblind GUis. WOuld it also be smart for us to take inspiration from how pre-colour monitors and monochrom,atic early computer printers rendered cponmtrast through pattern when colour ewasn't availbale/ Myabe non-colour modalities like e-papr will have inspiration. Do you understand the purpose and the vibe of the Monochrome skin I'm asking for?"*. Pattern stands in for hue; precedents he named: pre-colour monitors, early monochrome printers, e-paper. Desk reply: WCAG 1.4.1 (Level A) forbids colour as the ONLY visual means and names no technique, so MONOCHROME is one compliant answer and not the accessibility fix itself; redundant encoding belongs in every skin. Desk additions, unverified attribution: heraldic hatching (Petra Sancta, 17th c.) as a formal colour-to-texture system; and music engraving as the strongest precedent, since a printed score has never had colour. His colour-story rule survives intact as "a texture names one idea".
